Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the homologies structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 112. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 634 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the homologies decomposition. By minimality, the element a^112 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 634-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
